Convert window lengths to samples\n    sta_samples = int(sta_len * sampling_rate)\n    lta_samples = int(lta_len * sampling_rate)\n\n    # Initialize STA and LTA arrays\n    sta = np.zeros(n)\n    lta = np.zeros(n)\n\n    # Compute STA (short-term average)\n    for i in range(sta_samples, n):\n        sta[i] = np.mean(trace[i-sta_samples:i])\n\n    # Compute LTA (long-term average)\n    for i in range(lta_samples, n):\n        lta[i] = np.mean(trace[i-lta_samples:i])\n\n    # Avoid division by zero and very small LTA values\n    lta = np.maximum(lta, 1e-6)\n\n    # Compute STA/LTA ratio\n    sta_lta = np.divide(sta, lta)\n\n    # Handle initial samples where we don't have enough data\n    sta_lta[:lta_samples] = 0\n\n    return sta_lta\n\n\ndef find_peaks(characteristic_function, min_distance, threshold):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find peaks in the characteristic function above a threshold.\n\n    Args:\n        characteristic_function: Input characteristic function\n        min_distance: Minimum distance between peaks (in samples)\n        threshold: Minimum threshold for peaks\n\n    Returns:\n        peak_indices: Indices of detected peaks\n    \"\"\"\n    # If we have 3 channels, assume order is E, N, Z\n        if trace.shape[1] == 3:\n            z_idx = 2\n        else:\n            # Take first channel if not 3-channel\n            z_idx = 0\n\n        signal_trace = trace[:, z_idx]\n\n        # Use higher frequency content for P-waves\n        # Apply a bandpass filter to highlight P-wave frequencies\n        nyquist = sampling_rate / 2\n        low_freq = 1.0  # Hz\n        high_freq = min(20.0, nyquist - 1)  # Hz, but below Nyquist\n\n        # Design bandpass filter\n        if high_freq > low_freq:\n            b, a = signal.butter(4, [low_freq/nyquist, high_freq/nyquist], btype='band')\n            filtered_trace = signal.filtfilt(b, a, signal_trace)\n        else:\n            filtered_trace = signal_trace\n\n        # Use characteristic function based on absolute amplitude\n        cf = np.abs(filtered_trace)\n\n    else:  # S-wave\n        # Use horizontal components (E, N or combination)\n        if trace.shape[1] >= 2:\n            # Compute horizontal energy (sum of squares)\n            signal_trace = np.sqrt(h1**2 + h2**2)\n        else:\n            signal_trace = trace[:, 0]\n\n        # Use lower frequency content for S-waves\n        # S-waves typically have lower frequencies than P-waves\n        nyquist = sampling_rate / 2\n        low_freq = 0.5  # Hz\n        high_freq = min(10.0, nyquist - 1)  # Hz\n\n        if high_freq > low_freq:\n            b, a = signal.butter(4, [low_freq/nyquist, high_freq/nyquist], btype='band')\n            filtered_trace = signal.filtfilt(b, a, signal_trace)\n        else:\n            filtered_trace = signal_trace\n\n        # Use characteristic function based on amplitude\n        cf = np.abs(filtered_trace)\n\n    # Apply STA/LTA\n    if phase_type == 'P':\n        # P-waves: use shorter windows for faster response\n        sta_len = 0.5  # seconds\n        lta_len = 5.0  # seconds\n        threshold = 3.0  # threshold for detection\n        min_distance = int(0.5 * sampling_rate)  # minimum 0.5 seconds between picks\n    else:  # S-waves\n        # S-waves: use longer windows\n        sta_len = 1.0  # seconds\n        lta_len = 10.0  # seconds\n        threshold = 2.5  # threshold for detection\n        min_distance = int(1.0 * sampling_rate)  # minimum 1 second between picks\n\n    # Compute STA/LTA\n    sta_lta = compute_sta_lta(cf, sampling_rate, sta_len, lta_len)\n\n    # Find peaks\n    peak_indices = find_peaks(sta_lta, min_distance, threshold)\n\n    # Post-processing: remove picks in the first few seconds (noise)\n    # and last few seconds (edge effects)\n    min_start = int(5.0 * sampling_rate)  # ignore first 5 seconds\n    max_end = len(trace) - int(5.0 * sampling_rate)  # ignore last 5 seconds\n\n    valid_picks = [idx for idx in peak_indices if min_start <= idx < max_end]\n\n    # Additional filtering: ensure the pick is in a region of significant energy\n    # Use a sliding window to check local maximum\n    filtered_picks = []\n    window = int(0.2 * sampling_rate)  # If the local maximum is at or very close to our pick\n        if abs(local_max_idx + start - pick) <= window // 4:\n            filtered_picks.append(pick)\n\n    return filtered_picks\n\n\ndef process_trace(file_path):\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a single trace file and return P and S wave picks.\n\n    Args:\n        file_path: Path to the .npz file\n\n    Returns:\n        dict: Dictionary with file name and picks\n    \"\"\"\n    # ignore last 5 seconds\n\n    valid_picks = [idx for idx in peak_indices if min_start <= idx < max_end]\n\n    return valid_picks","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nSeismic Phase Picking using STA/LTA Algorithm\n\nThis script implements phase picking for P and S waves using the STA/LTA method.\nP-waves are picked using the vertical component (DPZ or HHZ or BHZ)\nS-waves are picked using horizontal components (DPE/DPN or HHE/HHN or BHE/BHN)\n\"\"\"\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom scipy import signal\n\n\ndef compute_sta_lta(trace, sampling_rate, sta_len, lta_len):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute STA/LTA characteristic function for phase picking.\n\n    Args:\n        trace: Input signal trace\n        sampling_rate: Sampling rate in Hz\n        sta_len: Length of short-term average window in seconds\n        lta_len: Length of long-term average window in seconds\n\n    Returns:\n        sta_lta: STA/LTA characteristic function\n    \"\"\"\n    n = len(trace)\n\n    # Convert window lengths to samples\n    sta_samples = int(sta_len * sampling_rate)\n    lta_samples = int(lta_len * sampling_rate)\n\n    # Initialize STA and LTA arrays\n    sta = np.zeros(n)\n    lta = np.zeros(n)\n\n    # Compute STA (short-term average)\n    for i in range(sta_samples, n):\n        sta[i] = np.mean(trace[i-sta_samples:i])\n\n    # Compute LTA (long-term average)\n    for i in range(lta_samples, n):\n        lta[i] = np.mean(trace[i-lta_samples:i])\n\n    # Avoid division by zero and very small LTA values\n    lta = np.maximum(lta, 1e-6)\n\n    # Compute STA/LTA ratio\n    sta_lta = np.divide(sta, lta)\n\n    # Handle initial samples where we don't have enough data\n    sta_lta[:lta_samples] = 0\n\n    return sta_lta\n\n\ndef find_peaks(characteristic_function, min_distance, threshold):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find peaks in the characteristic function above a threshold.\n\n    Args:\n        characteristic_function: Input characteristic function\n        min_distance: Minimum distance between peaks (in samples)\n        threshold: Minimum threshold for peaks\n\n    Returns:\n        peak_indices: Indices of detected peaks\n    \"\"\"\n    # Find peaks above threshold\n    peaks, _ = signal.find_peaks(\n        characteristic_function,\n        height=threshold,\n        distance=min_distance\n    )\n\n    return peaks\n\n\ndef pick_phases_sta_lta(trace, sampling_rate, phase_type='P'):\n    \"\"\"\n    Pick P or S phases using STA/LTA algorithm.\n\n    Args:\n        trace: Input seismic trace (samples x channels)\n        sampling_rate: Sampling rate in Hz\n        phase_type: 'P' for P-wave, 'S' for S-wave\n\n    Returns:\n        pick_indices: List of pick indices\n    \"\"\"\n    picks = []\n\n    # For P-wave picking, use vertical component\n    # For S-wave picking, use horizontal components\n    if phase_type == 'P':\n        # Use vertical component (usually the last channel: DPZ, HHZ, BHZ, etc.)\n        # Or use the one with Z in the name\n        z_idx = None\n        channels = ['Z', 'BHZ', 'HHZ', 'DPZ']\n        # For DPZ, it's typically the third channel (index 2)\n        # But we'll try to detect it\n\n        # If we have 3 channels, assume order is E, N, Z\n        if trace.shape[1] == 3:\n            z_idx = 2\n        else:\n            # Take first channel if not 3-channel\n            z_idx = 0\n\n        signal_trace = trace[:, z_idx]\n\n        # Use higher frequency content for P-waves\n        # Apply a bandpass filter to highlight P-wave frequencies\n        nyquist = sampling_rate / 2\n        low_freq = 1.0  # Hz\n        high_freq = min(20.0, nyquist - 1)  # Hz, but below Nyquist\n\n        # Design bandpass filter\n        if high_freq > low_freq:\n            b, a = signal.butter(4, [low_freq/nyquist, high_freq/nyquist], btype='band')\n            filtered_trace = signal.filtfilt(b, a, signal_trace)\n        else:\n            filtered_trace = signal_trace\n\n        # Use characteristic function based on absolute amplitude\n        cf = np.abs(filtered_trace)\n\n    else:  # S-wave\n        # Use horizontal components (E, N or combination)\n        if trace.shape[1] >= 2:\n            # Combine horizontal components\n            h1 = trace[:, 0]  # First horizontal component\n            h2 = trace[:, 1] if trace.shape[1] > 1 else trace[:, 0]  # Second horizontal or repeat\n\n            # Compute horizontal energy (sum of squares)\n            signal_trace = np.sqrt(h1**2 + h2**2)\n        else:\n            signal_trace = trace[:, 0]\n\n        # Use lower frequency content for S-waves\n        # S-waves typically have lower frequencies than P-waves\n        nyquist = sampling_rate / 2\n        low_freq = 0.5  # Hz\n        high_freq = min(10.0, nyquist - 1)  # Hz\n\n        if high_freq > low_freq:\n            b, a = signal.butter(4, [low_freq/nyquist, high_freq/nyquist], btype='band')\n            filtered_trace = signal.filtfilt(b, a, signal_trace)\n        else:\n            filtered_trace = signal_trace\n\n        # Use characteristic function based on amplitude\n        cf = np.abs(filtered_trace)\n\n    # Apply STA/LTA\n    if phase_type == 'P':\n        # P-waves: use shorter windows for faster response\n        sta_len = 0.5  # seconds\n        lta_len = 5.0  # seconds\n        threshold = 3.0  # threshold for detection\n        min_distance = int(0.5 * sampling_rate)  # minimum 0.5 seconds between picks\n    else:  # S-waves\n        # S-waves: use longer windows\n        sta_len = 1.0  # seconds\n        lta_len = 10.0  # seconds\n        threshold = 2.5  # threshold for detection\n        min_distance = int(1.0 * sampling_rate)  # minimum 1 second between picks\n\n    # Compute STA/LTA\n    sta_lta = compute_sta_lta(cf, sampling_rate, sta_len, lta_len)\n\n    # Find peaks\n    peak_indices = find_peaks(sta_lta, min_distance, threshold)\n\n    # Post-processing: remove picks in the first few seconds (noise)\n    # and last few seconds (edge effects)\n    min_start = int(5.0 * sampling_rate)  # ignore first 5 seconds\n    max_end = len(trace) - int(5.0 * sampling_rate)  # ignore last 5 seconds\n\n    valid_picks = [idx for idx in peak_indices if min_start <= idx < max_end]\n\n    # Additional filtering: ensure the pick is in a region of significant energy\n    # Use a sliding window to check local maximum\n    filtered_picks = []\n    window = int(0.2 * sampling_rate)  # 0.2 second window\n\n    for pick in valid_picks:\n        start = max(0, pick - window)\n        end = min(len(cf), pick + window)\n\n        # Check if this is a local maximum\n        local_cf = cf[start:end]\n        local_max_idx = np.argmax(local_cf)\n\n        # If the local maximum is at or very close to our pick\n        if abs(local_max_idx + start - pick) <= window // 4:\n            filtered_picks.append(pick)\n\n    return filtered_picks\n\n\ndef process_trace(file_path):\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a single trace file and return P and S wave picks.\n\n    Args:\n        file_path: Path to the .npz file\n\n    Returns:\n        dict: Dictionary with file name and picks\n    \"\"\"\n    # Load the data\n    data = np.load(file_path)\n\n    trace = data['data']\n    dt = data['dt']\n    sampling_rate = 1.0 / dt\n\n    # Get the file name\n    file_name = os.path.basename(file_path)\n\n    # Pick P-waves\n    p_picks = pick_phases_sta_lta(trace, sampling_rate, phase_type='P')\n\n    # Pick S-waves\n    s_picks = pick_phases_sta_lta(trace, sampling_rate, phase_type='S')\n\n    return {\n        'file_name': file_name,\n        'p_picks': p_picks,\n        's_picks': s_picks\n    }\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"\n    Main function to process all traces and generate results.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Define data directory\n    data_dir = Path('/root/data')\n    output_file = Path('/root/results.csv')\n\n    # Get all .npz files\n    npz_files = sorted(data_dir.glob('*.npz'))\n    print(f\"Found {len(npz_files)} trace files to process\")\n\n    # Process all files\n    all_results = []\n\n    for i, npz_file in enumerate(npz_files):\n        print(f\"Processing {i+1}/{len(npz_files)}: {npz_file.name}\")\n\n        try:\n            result = process_trace(npz_file)\n\n            # Add P picks to results\n            for pick_idx in result['p_picks']:\n                all_results.append({\n                    'file_name': result['file_name'],\n                    'phase': 'P',\n                    'pick_idx': pick_idx\n                })\n\n            # Add S picks to results\n            for pick_idx in result['s_picks']:\n                all_results.append({\n                    'file_name': result['file_name'],\n                    'phase': 'S',\n                    'pick_idx': pick_idx\n                })\n\n            print(f\"  Found {len(result['p_picks'])} P picks, {len(result['s_picks'])} S picks\")\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"  Error processing {npz_file.name}: {e}\")\n\n    # Create DataFrame and save to CSV\n    df = pd.DataFrame(all_results)\n\n    if len(df) > 0:\n        df.to_csv(output_file, index=False)\n        print(f\"\\nResults saved to {output_file}\")\n        print(f\"Total picks: {len(df)}\")\n        print(f\"P picks: {len(df[df['phase'] == 'P'])}\")\n        print(f\"S picks: {len(df[df['phase'] == 'S'])}\")\n    else:\n        print(\"No picks found!\")\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":175,"oldLines":33,"newStart":175,"newLines":15,"lines":["     # Convert window lengths to samples\n    sta_samples = int(sta_len * sampling_rate)\n    lta_samples = int(lta_len * sampling_rate)\n\n    # Initialize STA and LTA arrays\n    sta = np.zeros(n)\n    lta = np.zeros(n)\n\n    # Compute STA (short-term average)\n    for i in range(sta_samples, n):\n        sta[i] = np.mean(trace[i-sta_samples:i])\n\n    # Compute LTA (long-term average)\n    for i in range(lta_samples, n):\n        lta[i] = np.mean(trace[i-lta_samples:i])\n\n    # Avoid division by zero and very small LTA values\n    lta = np.maximum(lta, 1e-6)\n\n    # Compute STA/LTA ratio\n    sta_lta = np.divide(sta, lta)\n\n    # Handle initial samples where we don't have enough data\n    sta_lta[:lta_samples] = 0\n\n    return sta_lta\n\n\ndef find_peaks(characteristic_function, min_distance, threshold):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find peaks in the characteristic function above a threshold.\n\n    Args:\n        characteristic_function: Input characteristic function\n        min_distance: Minimum distance between peaks (in samples)\n        threshold: Minimum threshold for peaks\n\n    Returns:\n        peak_indices: Indices of detected peaks\n    \"\"\"\n    # Find peaks above threshold\n    peaks, _ = signal.find_peaks(\n        characteristic_function,\n        height=threshold,\n        distance=min_distance\n    )\n\n    return peaks\n\n\ndef pick_phases_sta_lta(trace, sampling_rate, phase_type='P'):\n    \"\"\"\n    Pick P or S phases using STA/LTA algorithm.\n\n    Args:\n        trace: Input seismic trace (samples x channels)\n        sampling_rate: Sampling rate in Hz\n        phase_type: 'P' for P-wave, 'S' for S-wave\n\n    Returns:\n        pick_indices: List of pick indices\n    \"\"\"\n    picks = []\n\n    # For P-wave picking, use vertical component\n    # For S-wave picking, use horizontal components\n    if phase_type == 'P':\n        # Use vertical component (usually the last channel: DPZ, HHZ, BHZ, etc.)\n        # Or use the one with Z in the name\n        z_idx = None\n        channels = ['Z', 'BHZ', 'HHZ', 'DPZ']\n        # For DPZ, it's typically the third channel (index 2)\n        # But we'll try to detect it\n\n        # If we have 3 channels, assume order is E, N, Z\n        if trace.shape[1] == 3:\n            z_idx = 2\n        else:\n            # Take first channel if not 3-channel\n            z_idx = 0\n\n        signal_trace = trace[:, z_idx]\n\n        # Use higher frequency content for P-waves\n        # Apply a bandpass filter to highlight P-wave frequencies\n        nyquist = sampling_rate / 2\n        low_freq = 1.0  # Hz\n        high_freq = min(20.0, nyquist - 1)  # Hz, but below Nyquist\n\n        # Design bandpass filter\n        if high_freq > low_freq:\n            b, a = signal.butter(4, [low_freq/nyquist, high_freq/nyquist], btype='band')\n            filtered_trace = signal.filtfilt(b, a, signal_trace)\n        else:\n            filtered_trace = signal_trace\n\n        # Use characteristic function based on absolute amplitude\n        cf = np.abs(filtered_trace)\n\n    else:  # S-wave\n        # Use horizontal components (E, N or combination)\n        if trace.shape[1] >= 2:\n            # Combine horizontal components\n            h1 = trace[:, 0]  # First horizontal component\n            h2 = trace[:, 1] if trace.shape[1] > 1 else trace[:, 0]  # Second horizontal or repeat\n\n            # Compute horizontal energy (sum of squares)\n            signal_trace = np.sqrt(h1**2 + h2**2)\n        else:\n            signal_trace = trace[:, 0]\n\n        # Use lower frequency content for S-waves\n        # S-waves typically have lower frequencies than P-waves\n        nyquist = sampling_rate / 2\n        low_freq = 0.5  # Hz\n        high_freq = min(10.0, nyquist - 1)  # Hz\n\n        if high_freq > low_freq:\n            b, a = signal.butter(4, [low_freq/nyquist, high_freq/nyquist], btype='band')\n            filtered_trace = signal.filtfilt(b, a, signal_trace)\n        else:\n            filtered_trace = signal_trace\n\n        # Use characteristic function based on amplitude\n        cf = np.abs(filtered_trace)\n\n    # Apply STA/LTA\n    if phase_type == 'P':\n        # P-waves: use shorter windows for faster response\n        sta_len = 0.5  # seconds\n        lta_len = 5.0  # seconds\n        threshold = 3.0  # threshold for detection\n        min_distance = int(0.5 * sampling_rate)  # minimum 0.5 seconds between picks\n    else:  # S-waves\n        # S-waves: use longer windows\n        sta_len = 1.0  # seconds\n        lta_len = 10.0  # seconds\n        threshold = 2.5  # threshold for detection\n        min_distance = int(1.0 * sampling_rate)  # minimum 1 second between picks\n\n    # Compute STA/LTA\n    sta_lta = compute_sta_lta(cf, sampling_rate, sta_len, lta_len)\n\n    # Find peaks\n    peak_indices = find_peaks(sta_lta, min_distance, threshold)\n\n    # Post-processing: remove picks in the first and last few seconds (edge effects)\n    min_start = int(5.0 * sampling_rate)  # ignore first 5 seconds\n    max_end = len(trace) - int(5.0 * sampling_rate)  # ignore last 5 seconds\n\n    valid_picks = [idx for idx in peak_indices if min_start <= idx < max_end]\n\n    return valid_picks\n\n\ndef process_trace(file_path):\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a single trace file and return P and S wave picks.\n\n    Args:\n        file_path: Path to the .npz file\n\n    Returns:\n        dict: Dictionary with file name and picks\n    \"\"\"\n    # Load the data\n    data = np.load(file_path)\n\n    trace = data['data']\n    dt = data['dt']\n    sampling_rate = 1.0 / dt\n\n    # Get the file name\n    file_name = os.path.basename(file_path)\n\n    # Pick P-waves\n    p_picks = pick_phases_sta_lta(trace, sampling_rate, phase_type='P')\n\n    # Pick S-waves\n    s_picks = pick_phases_sta_lta(trace, sampling_rate, phase_type='S')\n\n    return {\n        'file_name': file_name,\n        'p_picks': p_picks,\n        's_picks': s_picks\n    }\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"\n    Main function to process all traces and generate results.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Define data directory\n    data_dir = Path('/root/data')\n    output_file = Path('/root/results.csv')\n\n    # Get all .npz files\n    npz_files = sorted(data_dir.glob('*.npz'))\n    print(f\"Found {len(npz_files)} trace files to process\")\n\n    # Process all files\n    all_results = []\n\n    for i, npz_file in enumerate(npz_files):\n        print(f\"Processing {i+1}/{len(npz_files)}: {npz_file.name}\")\n\n        try:\n            result = process_trace(npz_file)\n\n            # Add P picks to results\n            for pick_idx in result['p_picks']:\n                all_results.append({\n                    'file_name': result['file_name'],\n                    'phase': 'P',\n                    'pick_idx': pick_idx\n                })\n\n            # Convert window lengths to samples\n    sta_samples = int(sta_len * sampling_rate)\n    lta_samples = int(lta_len * sampling_rate)\n\n    # Initialize STA and LTA arrays\n    sta = np.zeros(n)\n    lta = np.zeros(n)\n\n    # Compute STA (short-term average)\n    for i in range(sta_samples, n):\n        sta[i] = np.mean(trace[i-sta_samples:i])\n\n    # Compute LTA (long-term average)\n    for i in range(lta_samples, n):\n        lta[i] = np.mean(trace[i-lta_samples:i])\n\n    # Find peaks above threshold\n    peaks, _ = signal.find_peaks(\n        characteristic_function,\n        height=threshold,\n        distance=min_distance\n    )\n\n    return peaks\n\n\ndef pick_phases_sta_lta(trace, sampling_rate, phase_type='P'):\n    \"\"\"\n    Pick P or S phases using STA/LTA algorithm.\n\n    Args:\n        trace: Input seismic trace (samples x channels)\n        sampling_rate: Sampling rate in Hz\n        phase_type: 'P' for P-wave, 'S' for S-wave\n\n    Returns:\n        pick_indices: List of pick indices\n    \"\"\"\n    picks = []\n\n    # For P-wave picking, use vertical component\n    # For S-wave picking, use horizontal components\n    if phase_type == 'P':\n        # Use vertical component (usually the last channel: DPZ, HHZ, BHZ, etc.)\n        # Or use the one with Z in the name\n        z_idx = None\n        channels = ['Z', 'BHZ', 'HHZ', 'DPZ']\n        # For DPZ, it's typically the third channel (index 2)\n        # But we'll try to detect it\n\n        # If we have 3 channels, assume order is E, N, Z\n        if trace.shape[1] == 3:\n            z_idx = 2\n        else:\n            # Take first channel if not 3-channel\n            z_idx = 0\n\n        signal_trace = trace[:, z_idx]\n\n        # Use characteristic function based on absolute amplitude\n        # Skip bandpass filter for now to avoid signal loss\n        cf = np.abs(signal_trace)\n\n    else:  # S-wave\n        # Use horizontal components (E, N or combination)\n        if trace.shape[1] >= 2:\n            # Combine horizontal components\n            h1 = trace[:, 0]  # First horizontal component\n            h2 = trace[:, 1] if trace.shape[1] > 1 else trace[:, 0]  # Second horizontal or repeat\n\n            # Compute horizontal energy (sum of squares)\n            signal_trace = np.sqrt(h1**2 + h2**2)\n        else:\n            signal_trace = trace[:, 0]\n\n        # Use characteristic function based on amplitude\n        # Skip bandpass filter for now\n        cf = np.abs(signal_trace)\n\n    # Apply STA/LTA\n    if phase_type == 'P':\n        # P-waves: use shorter windows for faster response\n        sta_len = 0.5  # seconds\n        lta_len = 5.0  # seconds\n        threshold = 3.0  # threshold for detection\n        min_distance = int(0.5 * sampling_rate)  # minimum 0.5 seconds between picks\n    else:  # S-waves\n        # S-waves: use longer windows\n        sta_len = 1.0  # seconds\n        lta_len = 10.0  # seconds\n        threshold = 2.5  # threshold for detection\n        min_distance = int(1.0 * sampling_rate)  # minimum 1 second between picks\n\n    # Compute STA/LTA\n    sta_lta = compute_sta_lta(cf, sampling_rate, sta_len, lta_len)\n\n    # Find peaks\n    peak_indices = find_peaks(sta_lta, min_distance, threshold)\n\n    # Post-processing: remove picks in the first and last few seconds (edge effects)\n    min_start = int(5.0 * sampling_rate)  # ignore first 5 seconds\n    max_end = len(trace) - int(5.0 * sampling_rate)  # ignore last 5 seconds\n\n    valid_picks = [idx for idx in peak_indices if min_start <= idx < max_end]\n\n    return valid_picks\n\n\ndef process_trace(file_path):\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a single trace file and return P and S wave picks.\n\n    Args:\n        file_path: Path to the .npz file\n\n    Returns:\n        dict: Dictionary with file name and picks\n    \"\"\"\n    # Load the data\n    data = np.load(file_path)\n\n    trace = data['data']\n    dt = data['dt']\n    sampling_rate = 1.0 / dt\n\n    # Get the file name\n    file_name = os.path.basename(file_path)\n\n    # Pick P-waves\n    p_picks = pick_phases_sta_lta(trace, sampling_rate, phase_type='P')\n\n    # Pick S-waves\n    s_picks = pick_phases_sta_lta(trace, sampling_rate, phase_type='S')\n\n    return {\n        'file_name': file_name,\n        'p_picks': p_picks,\n        's_picks': s_picks\n    }\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"\n    Main function to process all traces and generate results.\n    \"\"\"\n    #
